- 39757 nodes · 69936 edges · 2023 communities (1520 shown, 247 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 92% EXTRACTED · 8% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5493 edges (avg confidence: 0.84)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output
